First view vision features `[B, N, D]` merged with text embeddings via `_merge_input_ids_with_image_features`\n5. Merged sequence → Florence2 encoder → `enc_out [B, T_enc, D]`\n\n\n6. Remaining views become auxiliary visual inputs `[B, (V-1)*N, D]`\n7. Returns both the encoded output and auxiliary inputs as a dictionary\n\n**`generate_actions` pipeline:**\n1. `forward_vlm` → enc dict\n2. Random noise → iterative denoising through `self.transformer` with flow matching\n3. Returns action predictions\n\n**Pruning strategy:**\nThe pruning should happen on the image features from `_encode_image()` BEFORE they're merged with text and sent to the Florence2 encoder. Multi-view image inputs (e.g., from multiple cameras).\\\\n      - Batch processing for multiple samples.\\\\n      - Joint tokenization and image tensor preparation.\\\\n\\\\n    This processor combines an image processor and a tokenizer under a single interface\\\\n    so that users can call it directly like:\\\\n\\\\n        >>> processor = XVLAProcessor.from_pretrained(\\\\\\\"path/to/xvla\\\\\\\")\\\\n        >>> inputs = processor(images=batch_images, language_instruction=batch_texts)\\\\n\\\\n    It is fully compatible with the Hugging Face AutoProcessor API.\\\\n\\\\n    Attributes\\\\n    ----------\\\\n    num_views : int, default=3\\\\n        Expected number of image views per sample. Missing views will be padded with zeros.\\\\n    language_max_length : int, default=50\\\\n        Maximum token length for text encoding.\\\\n    attributes : list\\\\n        Required by ProcessorMixin to know which submodules are stored and reloaded.\\\\n    image_processor_class : str\\\\n        The name of the associated image processor class.\\\\n    tokenizer_class : tuple(str)\\\\n        The names of compatible tokenizer classes.\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n    num_views: int = 3\\\\n    language_max_length: int = 50\\\\n\\\\n    # Hugging Face ProcessorMixin-required metadata\\\\n    attributes = [\\\\\\\"image_processor\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"tokenizer\\\\\\\"]\\\\n    image_processor_class = \\\\\\\"AutoImageProcessor\\\\\\\"\\\\n    tokenizer_class = (\\\\\\\"BartTokenizer\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"BartTokenizerFast\\\\\\\")\\\\n\\\\n    def __init__(self, image_processor=None, tokenizer=None):\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        Initialize XVLAProcessor.\\\\n\\\\n        Parameters\\\\n        ----------\\\\n        image_processor : PreTrainedImageProcessor, optional\\\\n            The image processor used to normalize/resize images.\\\\n        tokenizer : PreTrainedTokenizer, optional\\\\n            The tokenizer used for text tokenization.\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        # ProcessorMixin automatically saves these under self.image_processor / self.tokenizer\\\\n        super().__init__(image_processor, tokenizer)\\\\n\\\\n    # ================== LANGUAGE ENCODING ==================\\\\n    def encode_language(self, language_instruction: Union[str, List[str]]) -> Dict[str, torch.Tensor]:\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        Tokenize one or more language instructions.\\\\n\\\\n        Parameters\\\\n        ----------\\\\n        language_instruction : str or List[str]\\\\n            A single instruction or a batch of instructions.\\\\n\\\\n        Returns\\\\n        -------\\\\n        Dict[str, torch.Tensor]\\\\n            {\\\\n              \\\\\\\"input_ids\\\\\\\": tensor of shape [B, L]\\\\n            }\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        if isinstance(language_instruction, str):\\\\n            language_instruction = [language_instruction]\\\\n\\\\n        inputs = self.tokenizer(\\\\n            language_instruction,\\\\n            return_tensors=\\\\\\\"pt\\\\\\\",\\\\n            padding=\\\\\\\"max_length\\\\\\\",\\\\n            max_length=self.language_max_length,\\\\n            truncation=True,\\\\n        )\\\\n        return {\\\\\\\"input_ids\\\\\\\": inputs[\\\\\\\"input_ids\\\\\\\"]}\\\\n\\\\n    # ================== IMAGE ENCODING ==================\\\\n    def encode_image(\\\\n        self,\\\\n        images: Union[List, List[List]],\\\\n        **kwargs\\\\n    ) -> Dict[str, torch.Tensor]:\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        Preprocess one or more sets of multi-view images.\\\\n\\\\n        Parameters\\\\n        ----------\\\\n        images : List or List[List]\\\\n            Single sample: [img1, img2, ...]\\\\n            Batch: [[img1a, img1b], [img2a, img2b, img2c], ...]\\\\n            Each image may be a PIL.Image, NumPy array, or torch.Tensor.\\\\n\\\\n        kwargs : dict\\\\n            Extra arguments passed to the underlying image processor\\\\n            (e.g., `do_resize=False`, `size=(224,224)`).\\\\n\\\\n        Returns\\\\n        -------\\\\n        Dict[str, torch.Tensor]\\\\n            {\\\\n              \\\\\\\"image_input\\\\\\\": tensor [B, num_views, C, H, W],\\\\n              \\\\\\\"image_mask\\\\\\\": tensor [B, num_views]\\\\n            }\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        # Normalize to batch form\\\\n        if not isinstance(images[0], (list, tuple)):\\\\n            images = [images]  # convert single sample to batch of size 1\\\\n\\\\n        batch_imgs, batch_masks = [], []\\\\n\\\\n        for sample_imgs in images:\\\\n            processed = self.image_processor(sample_imgs, return_tensors=\\\\\\\"pt\\\\\\\", **kwargs)[\\\\\\\"pixel_values\\\\\\\"]\\\\n            V_exist = processed.size(0)\\\\n\\\\n            # Pad to self.num_views\\\\n            if V_exist < self.num_views:\\\\n                processed = torch.cat(\\\\n                    [processed,\\\\n                     processed.new_zeros(self.num_views - V_exist, *processed.shape[1:])],\\\\n                    dim=0,\\\\n                )\\\\n\\\\n            # Mask: True for valid slots, False for padding\\\\n            image_mask = torch.zeros(self.num_views, dtype=torch.bool, device=processed.device)\\\\n            image_mask[:V_exist] = True\\\\n\\\\n            batch_imgs.append(processed)\\\\n            batch_masks.append(image_mask)\\\\n\\\\n        image_input = torch.stack(batch_imgs, dim=0)  # [B, num_views, C, H, W]\\\\n        image_mask = torch.stack(batch_masks, dim=0)  # [B, num_views]\\\\n\\\\n        return {\\\\\\\"image_input\\\\\\\": image_input, \\\\\\\"image_mask\\\\\\\": image_mask}\\\\n\\\\n    # ================== COMBINED CALL ==================\\\\n    def __call__(\\\\n        self,\\\\n        images: Optional[Union[List, List[List]]] = None,\\\\n        language_instruction: Optional[Union[str, List[str]]] = None,\\\\n        **kwargs\\\\n    ) -> Dict[str, torch.Tensor]:\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        Combine image and text encoding into a unified multimodal input.\\\\n\\\\n        Parameters\\\\n        ----------\\\\n        images : List or List[List], optional\\\\n            Single-sample or batched multi-view images.\\\\n        language_instruction : str or List[str], optional\\\\n            Corresponding text instructions.\\\\n        kwargs : dict\\\\n            Extra args passed to image processor.\\\\n\\\\n        Returns\\\\n        -------\\\\n        Dict[str, torch.Tensor]\\\\n            {\\\\n              \\\\\\\"input_ids\\\\\\\": [B, L], optional,\\\\n              \\\\\\\"image_input\\\\\\\": [B, num_views, C, H, W], optional,\\\\n              \\\\\\\"image_mask\\\\\\\": [B, num_views], optional\\\\n            }\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        outputs: Dict[str, Any] = {}\\\\n\\\\n        # Encode language if provided\\\\n        if language_instruction is not None:\\\\n            outputs.update(self.encode_language(language_instruction))\\\\n\\\\n        # Encode image if provided\\\\n        if images is not None:\\\\n            outputs.update(self.encode_image(images, **kwargs))\\\\n\\\\n        # Sanity check for batch alignment\\\\n        if \\\\\\\"input_ids\\\\\\\" in outputs and \\\\\\\"image_input\\\\\\\" in outputs:\\\\n            assert outputs[\\\\\\\"input_ids\\\\\\\"].size(0) == outputs[\\\\\\\"image_input\\\\\\\"].size(0), (\\\\n                f\\\\\\\"Batch mismatch: text batch {outputs['input_ids'].size(0)} \\\\\\\"\\\\n ------------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n\\nfrom transformers import ProcessorMixin\\nfrom typing import List, Union, Dict, Any, Optional\\nimport torch\\n\\n\\nclass XVLAProcessor(ProcessorMixin):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    XVLAProcessor: Unified multimodal processor for XVLA models.\\n\\n    Handles:\\n      - Multi-view image inputs (e.g., from multiple cameras).\\n      - Batch processing for multiple samples.\\n      - Joint tokenization and image tensor preparation.\\n\\n    This processor combines an image processor and a tokenizer under a single interface\\n    so that users can call it directly like:\\n\\n        >>> processor = XVLAProcessor.from_pretrained(\\\"path/to/xvla\\\")\\n        >>> inputs = processor(images=batch_images, language_instruction=batch_texts)\\n\\n    It is fully compatible with the Hugging Face AutoProcessor API.\\n\\n    Attributes\\n    ----------\\n    num_views : int, default=3\\n        Expected number of image views per sample. Missing views will be padded with zeros.\\n    language_max_length : int, default=50\\n        Maximum token length for text encoding.\\n    attributes : list\\n        Required by ProcessorMixin to know which submodules are stored and reloaded.\\n    image_processor_class : str\\n        The name of the associated image processor class.\\n    tokenizer_class : tuple(str)\\n        The names of compatible tokenizer classes.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    num_views: int = 3\\n    language_max_length: int = 50\\n\\n    # Hugging Face ProcessorMixin-required metadata\\n    attributes = [\\\"image_processor\\\", \\\"tokenizer\\\"]\\n    image_processor_class = \\\"AutoImageProcessor\\\"\\n    tokenizer_class = (\\\"BartTokenizer\\\", \\\"BartTokenizerFast\\\")\\n\\n    def __init__(self, image_processor=None, tokenizer=None):\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        Initialize XVLAProcessor.\\n\\n        Parameters\\n        ----------\\n        image_processor : PreTrainedImageProcessor, optional\\n            The image processor used to normalize/resize images.\\n        tokenizer : PreTrainedTokenizer, optional\\n            The tokenizer used for text tokenization.\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        # ProcessorMixin automatically saves these under self.image_processor / self.tokenizer\\n        super().__init__(image_processor, tokenizer)\\n\\n    # ================== LANGUAGE ENCODING ==================\\n    def encode_language(self, language_instruction: Union[str, List[str]]) -> Dict[str, torch.Tensor]:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        Tokenize one or more language instructions.\\n\\n        Parameters\\n        ----------\\n        language_instruction : str or List[str]\\n            A single instruction or a batch of instructions.\\n\\n        Returns\\n        -------\\n        Dict[str, torch.Tensor]\\n            {\\n              \\\"input_ids\\\": tensor of shape [B, L]\\n            }\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        if isinstance(language_instruction, str):\\n            language_instruction = [language_instruction]\\n\\n        inputs = self.tokenizer(\\n            language_instruction,\\n            return_tensors=\\\"pt\\\",\\n            padding=\\\"max_length\\\",\\n            max_length=self.language_max_length,\\n            truncation=True,\\n        )\\n        return {\\\"input_ids\\\": inputs[\\\"input_ids\\\"]}\\n\\n    # ================== IMAGE ENCODING ==================\\n    def encode_image(\\n        self,\\n        images: Union[List, List[List]],\\n        **kwargs\\n    ) -> Dict[str, torch.Tensor]:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        Preprocess one or more sets of multi-view images.\\n\\n        Parameters\\n        ----------\\n        images : List or List[List]\\n            Single sample: [img1, img2, ...]\\n            Batch: [[img1a, img1b], [img2a, img2b, img2c], ...]\\n            Each image may be a PIL.Image, NumPy array, or torch.Tensor.\\n\\n        kwargs : dict\\n            Extra arguments passed to the underlying image processor\\n            (e.g., `do_resize=False`, `size=(224,224)`).\\n\\n        Returns\\n        -------\\n        Dict[str, torch.Tensor]\\n            {\\n              \\\"image_input\\\": tensor [B, num_views, C, H, W],\\n              \\\"image_mask\\\": tensor [B, num_views]\\n            }\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        # Normalize to batch form\\n        if not isinstance(images[0], (list, tuple)):\\n            images = [images]  # convert single sample to batch of size 1\\n\\n        batch_imgs, batch_masks = [], []\\n\\n        for sample_imgs in images:\\n            processed = self.image_processor(sample_imgs, return_tensors=\\\"pt\\\", **kwargs)[\\\"pixel_values\\\"]\\n            V_exist = processed.size(0)\\n\\n            # Pad to self.num_views\\n            if V_exist < self.num_views:\\n                processed = torch.cat(\\n                    [processed,\\n                     processed.new_zeros(self.num_views - V_exist, *processed.shape[1:])],\\n                    dim=0,\\n                )\\n\\n            # Mask: True for valid slots, False for padding\\n            image_mask = torch.zeros(self.num_views, dtype=torch.bool, device=processed.device)\\n            image_mask[:V_exist] = True\\n\\n            batch_imgs.append(processed)\\n            batch_masks.append(image_mask)\\n\\n        image_input = torch.stack(batch_imgs, dim=0)  # [B, num_views, C, H, W]\\n        image_mask = torch.stack(batch_masks, dim=0)  # [B, num_views]\\n\\n        return {\\\"image_input\\\": image_input, \\\"image_mask\\\": image_mask}\\n\\n    # ================== COMBINED CALL ==================\\n    def __call__(\\n        self,\\n        images: Optional[Union[List, List[List]]] = None,\\n        language_instruction: Optional[Union[str, List[str]]] = None,\\n        **kwargs\\n    ) -> Dict[str, torch.Tensor]:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        Combine image and text encoding into a unified multimodal input.\\n\\n        Parameters\\n        ----------\\n        images : List or List[List], optional\\n            Single-sample or batched multi-view images.\\n        language_instruction : str or List[str], optional\\n            Corresponding text instructions.\\n        kwargs : dict\\n            Extra args passed to image processor.\\n\\n        Returns\\n        -------\\n        Dict[str, torch.Tensor]\\n            {\\n              \\\"input_ids\\\": [B, L], optional,\\n              \\\"image_input\\\": [B, num_views, C, H, W], optional,\\n              \\\"image_mask\\\": [B, num_views], optional\\n            }\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        outputs: Dict[str, Any] = {}\\n\\n        # Encode language if provided\\n        if language_instruction is not None:\\n            outputs.update(self.encode_language(language_instruction))\\n\\n        # Encode image if provided\\n        if images is not None:\\n            outputs.update(self.encode_image(images, **kwargs))\\n\\n        # Sanity check for batch alignment\\n        if \\\"input_ids\\\" in outputs and \\\"image_input\\\" in outputs:\\n            assert outputs[\\\"input_ids\\\"].size(0) == outputs[\\\"image_input\\\"].size(0), (\\n                f\\\"Batch mismatch: text batch {outputs['input_ids'].size(0)} \\\"\\n                f\\\"!= image batch {outputs['image_input'].size(0)}\\\"\\n            )\\n        return outputs\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.43}}"}}}

Available: {list(ACTION_REGISTRY.keys())}\\\\\\\")\\\\n    return ACTION_REGISTRY[key](**kwargs)\\\\n\\\\n\\\\n# =============================================================================\\\\n# Base class\\\\n# =============================================================================\\\\nclass BaseActionSpace(nn.Module):\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    Abstract base class for all action-space definitions.\\\\n\\\\n    Each subclass defines:\\\\n      - `dim_action`: dimension of the action vector.\\\\n      - `gripper_idx`: indices of gripper channels.\\\\n      - `compute_loss(pred, target)`: supervised loss for this space.\\\\n      - `preprocess(proprio, action, mode)`: pre-step modifications.\\\\n      - `postprocess(action)`: post-step corrections (e.g. apply sigmoid).\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n    name: str = \\\\\\\"base\\\\\\\"\\\\n    dim_action: int = 0\\\\n    gripper_idx: Tuple[int, ...] = ()\\\\n\\\\n    def __init__(self):\\\\n        super().__init__()\\\\n\\\\n    # ---------------------------------------------------------------------\\\\n    # Core supervised loss\\\\n    # ---------------------------------------------------------------------\\\\n    def compute_loss(self, pred: torch.Tensor, target: torch.Tensor) -> Dict[str, torch.Tensor]:\\\\n        raise NotImplementedError\\\\n\\\\n    def forward(self, pred: torch.Tensor, target: torch.Tensor) -> Dict[str, torch.Tensor]:\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Alias for compute_loss.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        return self.compute_loss(pred, target)\\\\n\\\\n    # gi]) for gi in self.gripper_idx]\\\\n        gripper_loss = sum(g_losses) / len(self.gripper_idx) * self.GRIPPER_SCALE\\\\n\\\\n        joints_idx = tuple(i for i in range(D) if i not in ------------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n\\nfrom __future__ import annotations\\nfrom typing import Iterable, Tuple, Dict, Type\\nimport torch\\nimport torch.nn as nn\\n\\n# =============================================================================\\n# Registry\\n# =============================================================================\\nACTION_REGISTRY: Dict[str, Type[\\\"BaseActionSpace\\\"]] = {}\\n\\n\\ndef register_action(name: str):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Decorator for registering a new action space.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    def _wrap(cls):\\n        key = name.lower()\\n        if key in ACTION_REGISTRY:\\n            raise KeyError(f\\\"ActionSpace '{key}' already registered -> {ACTION_REGISTRY[key]}\\\")\\n        ACTION_REGISTRY[key] = cls\\n        cls.name = key\\n        return cls\\n    return _wrap\\n\\n\\ndef build_action_space(name: str, **kwargs) -> \\\"BaseActionSpace\\\":\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Instantiate a registered action space by name.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    key = name.lower()\\n    if key not in ACTION_REGISTRY:\\n        raise KeyError(f\\\"Unknown action space '{name}'. Available: {list(ACTION_REGISTRY.keys())}\\\")\\n    return ACTION_REGISTRY[key](**kwargs)\\n\\n\\n# =============================================================================\\n# Base class\\n# =============================================================================\\nclass BaseActionSpace(nn.Module):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    Abstract base class for all action-space definitions.\\n\\n    Each subclass defines:\\n      - `dim_action`: dimension of the action vector.\\n      - `gripper_idx`: indices of gripper channels.\\n      - `compute_loss(pred, target)`: supervised loss for this space.\\n      - `preprocess(proprio, action, mode)`: pre-step modifications.\\n      - `postprocess(action)`: post-step corrections (e.g. apply sigmoid).\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    name: str = \\\"base\\\"\\n    dim_action: int = 0\\n    gripper_idx: Tuple[int, ...] = ()\\n\\n    def __init__(self):\\n        super().__init__()\\n\\n    # ---------------------------------------------------------------------\\n    # Core supervised loss\\n    # ---------------------------------------------------------------------\\n    def compute_loss(self, pred: torch.Tensor, target: torch.Tensor) -> Dict[str, torch.Tensor]:\\n        raise NotImplementedError\\n\\n    def forward(self, pred: torch.Tensor, target: torch.Tensor) -> Dict[str, torch.Tensor]:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Alias for compute_loss.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        return self.compute_loss(pred, target)\\n\\n    # ---------------------------------------------------------------------\\n    # Space-level hooks\\n    # ---------------------------------------------------------------------\\n    def preprocess(\\n        self,\\n        proprio: torch.Tensor,\\n        action: torch.Tensor,\\n        mode: str = \\\"train\\\",\\n    ) -> Tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Default: return unchanged.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        return proprio, action\\n\\n    def postprocess(self, action: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Default: return unchanged.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        return action\\n\\n\\n# =============================================================================\\n# Utilities\\n# =============================================================================\\ndef _ensure_indices_valid(D: int, idx: Iterable[int], name: str) -> None:\\n    bad = [i for i in idx if i < 0 or i >= D]\\n    if bad:\\n        raise IndexError(f\\\"{name} contains out-of-range indices {bad} for action dim D={D}\\\")\\n\\n\\n#\\n\\n... [截断: 输出过长，省略 1865 字符] ...\\n\\n     action_m = action.clone()\\n        proprio_m[..., self.gripper_idx] = 0.0\\n        action_m[..., self.gripper_idx] = 0.0\\n        return proprio_m, action_m\\n\\n    def postprocess(self, action: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Apply sigmoid to gripper logits.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        if action.size(-1) > max(self.gripper_idx):\\n            action[..., self.gripper_idx] = torch.sigmoid(action[..., self.gripper_idx])\\n        return action\\n\\n\\n@register_action(\\\"joint\\\")\\nclass JointActionSpace(BaseActionSpace):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Joint-space layout with joints + gripper only.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    dim_action = 14\\n    gripper_idx = (6, 13)\\n    GRIPPER_SCALE = 0.1\\n    JOINTS_SCALE = 1.0\\n\\n    def __init__(self):\\n        super().__init__()\\n        self.mse = nn.MSELoss()\\n        self.bce = nn.BCEWithLogitsLoss()\\n\\n    def compute_loss(self, pred, target):\\n        assert pred.shape == target.shape\\n        B, T, D = pred.shape\\n        _ensure_indices_valid(D, self.gripper_idx, \\\"gripper_idx\\\")\\n\\n        g_losses = [self.bce(pred[:, :, gi], target[:, :, gi]) for gi in self.gripper_idx]\\n        gripper_loss = sum(g_losses) / len(self.gripper_idx) * self.GRIPPER_SCALE\\n\\n        joints_idx = tuple(i for i in range(D) if i not in set(self.gripper_idx))\\n        joints_loss = self.mse(pred[:, :, joints_idx], target[:, :, joints_idx]) * self.JOINTS_SCALE\\n\\n        return {\\n            \\\"joints_loss\\\": joints_loss,\\n            \\\"gripper_loss\\\": gripper_loss,\\n        }\\n\\n    def preprocess(self, proprio, action, mode=\\\"train\\\"):\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Zero-out gripper channels in proprio/action.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        proprio_m = proprio.clone()\\n        action_m = action.clone()\\n        proprio_m[..., self.gripper_idx] = 0.0\\n        action_m[..., self.gripper_idx] = 0.0\\n        return proprio_m, action_m\\n\\n    def postprocess(self, action: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Apply sigmoid to gripper logits.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        if action.size(-1) > max(self.gripper_idx):\\n            action[..., self.gripper_idx] = torch.sigmoid(action[..., self.gripper_idx])\\n        return action\\n\\n\\n@register_action(\\\"agibot_ee6d\\\")\\nclass AGIBOTEE6DActionSpace(BaseActionSpace):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"AGI-bot variant of EE6DActionSpace using MSE for all components.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    dim_action = 20\\n    gripper_idx = (9, 19)\\n    GRIPPER_SCALE = 10.0\\n    XYZ_SCALE = 100.0\\n    ROT_SCALE = 10.0\\n    POS_IDX_1 = (0, 1, 2)\\n    POS_IDX_2 = (10, 11, 12)\\n    ROT_IDX_1 = (3, 4, 5, 6, 7, 8)\\n    ROT_IDX_2 = (13, 14, 15, 16, 17, 18)\\n\\n    def __init__(self):\\n        super().__init__()\\n        self.mse = nn.MSELoss()\\n\\n    def compute_loss(self, pred, target):\\n        assert pred.shape == target.shape\\n        B, T, D = pred.shape\\n        _ensure_indices_valid(D, self.gripper_idx, \\\"gripper_idx\\\")\\n\\n        gripper_loss = self.mse(pred[:, :, self.gripper_idx], target[:, :, self.gripper_idx]) * self.GRIPPER_SCALE\\n        pos_loss = (\\n            self.mse(pred[:, :, self.POS_IDX_1], target[:, :, self.POS_IDX_1]) +\\n            self.mse(pred[:, :, self.POS_IDX_2], target[:, :, self.POS_IDX_2])\\n        ) * self.XYZ_SCALE\\n        rot_loss = (\\n            self.mse(pred[:, :, self.ROT_IDX_1], target[:, :, self.ROT_IDX_1]) +\\n            self.mse(pred[:, :, self.ROT_IDX_2], target[:, :, self.ROT_IDX_2])\\n        ) * self.ROT_SCALE\\n\\n        return {\\n            \\\"position_loss\\\": pos_loss,\\n            \\\"rotate6D_loss\\\": rot_loss,\\n            \\\"gripper_loss\\\": gripper_loss,\\n        }\\n\\n    def preprocess(self, proprio, action, mode=\\\"train\\\"):\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"No preprocessing applied in AGIBOT variant.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        return proprio, action\\n\\n    def postprocess(self, action: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"AGIBOT does not postprocess.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        return action\\n\\n\\n# =============================================================================\\n# Exports\\n# =============================================================================\\n__all__ = [\\n    \\\"BaseActionSpace\\\",\\n    \\\"build_action_space\\\",\\n    \\\"register_action\\\",\\n    \\\"EE6DActionSpace\\\",\\n    \\\"JointActionSpace\\\",\\n    \\\"AGIBOTEE6DActionSpace\\\",\\n    \\\"ACTION_REGISTRY\\\",\\n]\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.44}}"}}}

{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01T4Ukx6cg3pLe5BHhUBR4ER","type":"tool_result","content":"{\"result\":\"{\\\"success\\\": true, \\\"data\\\": {\\\"stdout\\\": \\\"---\\\\nlicense: apache-2.0\\\\nbase_model:\\\\n- microsoft/Florence-2-large\\\\ntags:\\\\n- robotics\\\\n- vla\\\\npipeline_tag: robotics\\\\n---\\\\n\\\\n#  X-VLA 0.9B (Libero Edition)\\\\n\\\\n\\\\n**Repository:** [2toINF/X-VLA](https://github.com/2toinf/X-VLA)\\\\n\\\\n**Authors:** [2toINF](https://github.com/2toINF) | **License:** Apache 2.0\\\\n\\\\n**Paper:** *Zheng et al., 2025, “X-VLA: Soft-Prompted Transformer as Scalable Cross-Embodiment Vision-Language-Action Model”* ([arXiv:2510.10274](https://arxiv.org/pdf/2510.10274))\\\\n\\\\n\\\\n## 🚀 Overview\\\\n\\\\nSuccessful generalist **Vision-Language-Action (VLA)** models rely on effective training across diverse robotic platforms with large-scale, cross-embodiment, heterogeneous datasets.\\\\nTo facilitate and leverage the heterogeneity in rich robotic data sources, **X-VLA** introduces a **Soft Prompt approach** with minimally added parameters: we infuse prompt-learning concepts into cross-embodiment robot learning, introducing **separate sets of learnable embeddings** for each distinct embodiment.\\\\n\\\\nThese embodiment-specific prompts empower VLA models to exploit cross-embodiment features effectively.\\\\nOur architecture—**a clean, flow-matching-based VLA design relying exclusively on soft-prompted standard Transformers**—achieves superior scalability and simplicity.\\\\n\\\\nTrained on **Bridge Data** and evaluated across **six simulations** and **three real-world robots**, the 0.9B-parameter X-VLA simultaneously achieves **state-of-the-art performance** across diverse benchmarks, demonstrating flexible dexterity and fast adaptation across embodiments, environments, and tasks.\\\\n\\\\n🌐 **Project Website:** [https://thu-air-dream.github.io/X-VLA/](https://thu-air-dream.github.io/X-VLA/)\\\\n\\\\n\\\\n&lt;video controls autoplay loop muted playsinline width=\\\\\\\"720\\\\\\\">\\\\n  &lt;source src=\\\\\\\"https://huggingface.co/2toINF/X-VLA-0.9B-WidowX/resolve/main/demo.mp4\\\\\\\" type=\\\\\\\"video/mp4\\\\\\\">\\\\n&lt;/video>\\\\n\\\\n## ⚙️ Usage\\\\n### 🔹 Load the model\\\\n\\\\n```python\\\\nfrom transformers import AutoModel\\\\n\\\\nmodel = AutoModel.from_pretrained(\\\\n    \\\\\\\"2toINF/X-VLA-WidowX\\\\\\\",\\\\n    trust_remote_code=True\\\\n)\\\\n```\\\\n### 🔹 Start FastAPI server\\\\n\\\\n```python\\\\nfrom transformers import AutoProcessor\\\\nprocessor = AutoProcessor.from_pretrained(\\\\\\\"2toINF/X-VLA-WidowX\\\\\\\", trust_remote_code=True)\\\\nmodel.run(processor, host=\\\\\\\"0.0.0.0\\\\\\\", port=8000)\\\\n```\\\\n### 🔹 Client-server evaluation\\\\n\\\\nYou can run the provided evaluation client from our GitHub:\\\\n👉 [2toINF/X-VLA – Client &amp; Server Code](https://github.com/2toINF/X-VLA)\\\\n\\\\n\\\\n## 🧩 Architecture\\\\n\\\\n| Component                         | Role                                                                       |\\\\n| :-------------------------------- | :------------------------------------------------------------------------- |\\\\n| **Florence 2 Encoder**      | Vision-Language representation backbone (encoder-only).                    |\\\\n| **SoftPromptedTransformer** | Flow-matching action denoiser using learnable soft prompts per embodiment. |\\\\n| **Action Hub**              | Defines action spaces, masking rules, pre/post-processing, and losses.     |\\\\n\\\\n## 🧠 Training Summary\\\\n\\\\n| Setting           | Value                                           |\\\\n| :---------------- | :---------------------------------------------- |\\\\n| Training Data     | Bridge Data V2    |\\\\n| Parameters        | ≈ 0.9 B                                        |\\\\n| Action Mode       | `ee6d`                                         |\\\\n| Precision         | BP16                                            |\\\\n| Framework         | PyTorch + Transformers platforms with large-scale, cross-embodiment, heterogeneous datasets.\\nTo facilitate and leverage the heterogeneity in rich robotic data sources, **X-VLA** introduces a **Soft Prompt approach** with minimally added parameters: we infuse prompt-learning concepts into cross-embodiment robot learning, introducing **separate sets of learnable embeddings** for each distinct embodiment.\\n\\nThese embodiment-specific prompts empower VLA models to exploit cross-embodiment features effectively.\\nOur architecture—**a clean, flow-matching-based VLA design relying exclusively on soft-prompted standard Transformers**—achieves superior scalability and simplicity.\\n\\nTrained on **Bridge Data** and evaluated across **six simulations** and **three real-world robots**, the 0.9B-parameter X-VLA simultaneously achieves **state-of-the-art performance** across diverse benchmarks, demonstrating flexible dexterity and fast adaptation across embodiments, environments, and tasks.\\n\\n🌐 **Project Website:** [https://thu-air-dream.github.io/X-VLA/](https://thu-air-dream.github.io/X-VLA/)\\n\\n\\n&lt;video controls autoplay loop muted playsinline width=\\\"720\\\">\\n  &lt;source src=\\\"https://huggingface.co/2toINF/X-VLA-0.9B-WidowX/resolve/main/demo.mp4\\\" type=\\\"video/mp4\\\">\\n&lt;/video>\\n\\n## ⚙️ Usage\\n### 🔹 Load the model\\n\\n```python\\nfrom transformers import AutoModel\\n\\nmodel = AutoModel.from_pretrained(\\n    \\\"2toINF/X-VLA-WidowX\\\",\\n    trust_remote_code=True\\n)\\n```\\n### 🔹 Start FastAPI server\\n\\n```python\\nfrom transformers import AutoProcessor\\nprocessor = AutoProcessor.from_pretrained(\\\"2toINF/X-VLA-WidowX\\\", trust_remote_code=True)\\nmodel.run(processor, host=\\\"0.0.0.0\\\", port=8000)\\n```\\n### 🔹 Client-server evaluation\\n\\nYou can run the provided evaluation client from our GitHub:\\n👉 [2toINF/X-VLA – Client &amp; Server Code](https://github.com/2toINF/X-VLA)\\n\\n\\n## 🧩 Architecture\\n\\n| Component                         | Role                                                                       |\\n| :-------------------------------- | :------------------------------------------------------------------------- |\\n| **Florence 2 Encoder**      | Vision-Language representation backbone (encoder-only).                    |\\n| **SoftPromptedTransformer** | Flow-matching action denoiser using learnable soft prompts per embodiment. |\\n| **Action Hub**              | Defines action spaces, masking rules, pre/post-processing, and losses.     |\\n\\n## 🧠 Training Summary\\n\\n| Setting           | Value                                           |\\n| :---------------- | :---------------------------------------------- |\\n| Training Data     | Bridge Data V2    |\\n| Parameters        | ≈ 0.9 B                                        |\\n| Action Mode       | `ee6d`                                         |\\n| Precision         | BP16                                            |\\n| Framework         | PyTorch + Transformers                          |\\n\\n---\\n## 🪪 License\\n```\\nCopyright 2025 2toINF Vision-Language-Action Model},\\n  author  = {Zheng, Jinliang and Li, Jianxiong and Wang, Zhihao and Liu, Dongxiu and Kang, Xirui\\n             and Feng, Yuchun and Zheng, Yinan and Zou, Jiayin and Chen, Yilun and Zeng, Jia and others},\\n  journal = {arXiv preprint arXiv:2510.10274},\\n  year    = {2025}\\n}\\n```\\n---\\n## 🌐 Links\\n\\n- 📄 **Paper:** [arXiv {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01JNYnf7H8sSzcNUapiBwfv3","type":"tool_result","content":"{\"result\":\"{\\\"success\\\": true, \\\"data\\\": {\\\"stdout\\\": \\\"    if not missing and not unexpected:\\\\n        log.info(\\\\\\\"Checkpoint loaded with strict match (no missing/unexpected keys)\\\\\\\")\\\\n    wrapper.eval()\\\\n\\\\n    log.info(\\\\\\\"SmartCompressWrapper loaded: M=%d, compressor params=%d\\\\\\\",\\\\n             args.n_queries,\\\\n             sum(p.numel() for p in wrapper.compressor.parameters()))\\\\n\\\\n    return wrapper, config\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef quat_to_axis_angle(quat):\\\\n    q = quat.astype(np.float64)\\\\n    w = np.clip(q[3], -1.0, 1.0)\\\\n    den = np.sqrt(max(1.0 - w * w, 0.0))\\\\n    if den > 1e-10:\\\\n        angle = 2.0 * np.arccos(w)\\\\n        axis = q[:3] / den\\\\n        return (axis * angle).astype(np.float32)\\\\n    return np.zeros(3, dtype=np.float32)\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef extract_state(obs):\\\\n    rs = obs[\\\\\\\"robot_state\\\\\\\"]\\\\n    eef_pos = np.asarray(rs[\\\\\\\"eef\\\\\\\"][\\\\\\\"pos\\\\\\\"], dtype=np.float32)\\\\n    eef_quat = np.asarray(rs[\\\\\\\"eef\\\\\\\"][\\\\\\\"quat\\\\\\\"], dtype=np.float64)\\\\n    gripper_qpos = np.asarray(rs[\\\\\\\"gripper\\\\\\\"][\\\\\\\"qpos\\\\\\\"], dtype=np.float32)\\\\n    eef_aa = quat_to_axis_angle(eef_quat)\\\\n    return np.concatenate([eef_pos, eef_aa, gripper_qpos])\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef obs_to_batch(obs, task_text, tokenizer, camera_keys, device):\\\\n    batch = {}\\\\n    for cam_key in camera_keys:\\\\n        short_name = cam_key.split(\\\\\\\".\\\\\\\")[-1]\\\\n        img = obs[\\\\\\\"pixels\\\\\\\"][short_name]\\\\n        img_t = torch.from_numpy(np.asarray(img)).permute(2, 0, 1).float() / 255.0\\\\n        img_t = img_t.unsqueeze(0)\\\\n        img_t = torch.flip(img_t, dims=[2, 3])\\\\n        batch[cam_key] = img_t.to(device)\\\\n\\\\n    state = extract_state(obs)\\\\n    batch[\\\\\\\"observation.state\\\\\\\"] = torch.from_numpy(state).unsqueeze(0).to(device)\\\\n\\\\n    text = task_text if task_text.endswith(\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\") else task_text + \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\"\\\\n    encoded = tokenizer(\\\\n        [text], padding=\\\\\\\"longest\\\\\\\", max_length=48,\\\\n        truncation=True, return_tensors=\\\\\\\"pt\\\\\\\",\\\\n    )\\\\n    batch[\\\\\\\"observation.language.tokens\\\\\\\"] = encoded[\\\\\\\"input_ids\\\\\\\"].to(device)\\\\n    batch[\\\\\\\"observation.language.attention_mask\\\\\\\"] = encoded[\\\\\\\"attention_mask\\\\\\\"].bool().to(device)\\\\n\\\\n    return batch\\\\n\\\\n\\\\n@torch.no_grad()\\\\ndef predict_actions_compressed(wrapper, batch):\\\\n    actions = wrapper.forward_inference(batch)\\\\n    action_dim = wrapper.policy.config.action_feature.shape[0]\\\\n    return actions[0, :, :action_dim]\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef wilson_ci(successes, total, z=1.96):\\\\n    if total == 0:\\\\n        return 0.0, 0.0, 1.0\\\\n    p = successes / total\\\\n    denom = 1 + z ** 2 / total\\\\n    center = (p + z ** 2 / (2 * total)) / denom\\\\n    spread = z * np.sqrt((p * (1 - p) + z ** 2 / (4 * total)) / total) / denom\\\\n    return center, max(0.0, center - spread), min(1.0, center + spread)\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef run_evaluation(args):\\\\n    device = torch.device(f\\\\\\\"cuda:{args.gpu}\\\\\\\" if torch.cuda.is_available() else \\\\\\\"cpu\\\\\\\")\\\\n    max_steps = args.max_steps %H:%M:%S\",\n)\nlog = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n\nSUITE_MAX_STEPS = {\n    \"libero_spatial\": 280,\n    \"libero_object\": 280,\n    \"libero_goal\": 300,\n    \"libero_10\": 520,\n    \"libero_90\": 400,\n}\n\n\ndef parse_args():\n    p = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=\"X-VLA pruning eval on LIBERO\")\n    p.add_argument(\"--model_path\", type=str, default=\"2toINF/X-VLA-Libero\",\n                   help=\"HuggingFace model path or local dir\")\n    p.add_argument(\"--suite\", type=str, default=\"libero_object\",\n                   choices=list(SUITE_MAX_STEPS.keys()))\n    p.add_argument(\"--keep_ratio\", type=float, default=1.0,\n                   help=\"Fraction of vision tokens to keep (1.0 = no pruning)\")\n    p.add_argument(\"--num_episodes\", type=int, default=50)\n    p.add_argument(\"--gpu\", type=int, default=0)\n    p.add_argument(\"--seed\", type=int, default=42)\n    p.add_argument(\"--action_horizon\", type=int, default=10,\n                   help=\"How many actions from each chunk to execute before re-planning\")\n    p.add_argument(\"--max_steps\", type=int, default=None)\n    p.add_argument(\"--output\", type=str, default=None)\n    p.add_argument(\"--task_ids\", type=str, default=None)\n    p.add_argument(\"--domain_id\", type=int, default=0)\n    p.add_argument(\"--denoise_steps\", type=int, default=10,\n                   help=\"Flow matching denoising steps\")\n    return p.parse_args()\n\n\n# --- Pruning hook ---\n\ndef install_vision_pruning(model, keep_ratio: float):\n    \"\"\"Monkey-patch _encode_image to prune vision tokens by L2 norm.\"\"\"\n    if keep_ratio >= 1.0:\n        log.info(\"keep_ratio=%.2f, no pruning applied\", keep_ratio)\n        return\n\n    original_encode = model.vlm._encode_image\n\n    @wraps(original_encode)\n    def pruned_encode(pixel_values):\n        features = original_encode(pixel_values)  # [B, N, D]\n        N = features.shape[1]\n        k = max(1, int(keep_ratio * N))\n        if k >= N:\n            return features\n        norms = features.norm(dim=-1)  # [B, N]\n        _, indices = norms.topk(k, dim=-1)\n        indices_sorted = indices.sort(dim=-1).values\n        pruned = features.gather(\n            1, indices_sorted.unsqueeze(-1).expand(-1, -1, features.shape[-1])\n        )\n        return pruned\n\n    model.vlm._encode_image = pruned_encode\n    log.info(\"Vision pruning installed: keep_ratio=%.2f\", keep_ratio)\n\n\n# --- Model loading ---\n\ndef load_model(model_path: str, device: torch.device):\n    from transformers import AutoModel, AutoProcessor\n\n    log.info(\"Loading X-VLA from %s\", model_path)\n    model = AutoModel.from_pretrained(\n        model_path,\n        torch_dtype=torch.float32,\n        trust_remote_code=True,\n        low_cpu_mem_usage=True,\n    )\n    model = model.to(device).eval()\n    processor = AutoProcessor.from_pretrained(model_path, trust_remote_code=True)\n\n    n_params = sum(p.numel() for p in model.parameters())\n    log.info(\"Model loaded: %.1fM params, dtype=%s\", n_params / 1e6, model.dtype)\n    return model, processor\n\n\n# --- Observation processing ---\n\ndef quat_to_axis_angle(quat):\n    q = quat.astype(np.float64)\n    w = np.clip(q[3], -1.0, 1.0)\n    den = np.sqrt(max(1.0 - w * w, 0.0))\n    if den > 1e-10:\n        angle = 2.0 * np.arccos(w)\n        axis = q[:3] / den\n        return (axis * angle).astype(np.float32)\n    return np.zeros(3, dtype=np.float32)\n\n\ndef extract_proprio(obs):\n    \"\"\"Extract 7D proprio state: [pos(3), axis_angle(3), gripper(1)].\"\"\"\n    rs = obs[\"robot_state\"]\n    eef_pos = np.asarray(rs[\"eef\"][\"pos\"], dtype=np.float32)\n    eef_quat = np.asarray(rs[\"eef\"][\"quat\"], dtype=np.float64)\n    gripper_qpos = np.asarray(rs[\"gripper\"][\"qpos\"], dtype=np.float32)\n    eef_aa = quat_to_axis_angle(eef_quat)\n    return np.concatenate([eef_pos, eef_aa, gripper_qpos[:1]])\n\n\ndef get_images_from_obs(obs):\n    \"\"\"Extract camera images from LIBERO observation.\"\"\"\n    images = []\n    for cam_name in [\"agentview_image\", \"robot0_eye_in_hand_image\"]:\n        img = obs.get(\"pixels\", {}).get(cam_name, None)\n        if img is None:\n            for k, v in obs.items():\n                if cam_name in k and isinstance(v, np.ndarray):\n                    img = v\n                    break\n        if img is not None:\n            if img.dtype in (np.float32, np.float64):\n                img = (np.clip(img, 0, 1) * 255).astype(np.uint8)\n            images.append(Image.fromarray(img))\n    return images\n\n\n# --- Action prediction ---\n\n@torch.no_grad()\ndef predict_actions(model, processor, images, task_desc, proprio, domain_id, device,\n                    denoise_steps=10):\n    \"\"\"Get action chunk from X-VLA model.\"\"\"\n    inputs = processor(images=images, language_instruction=task_desc)\n\n    proprio_t = torch.tensor(proprio, dtype=torch.float32).unsqueeze(0).to(device)\n    domain_t = torch.tensor([domain_id], dtype=torch.long).to(device)\n\n    for k, v in inputs.items():\n        if isinstance(v, torch.Tensor):\n            if v.is_floating_point():\n                inputs[k] = v.to(device=device, dtype=torch.float32)\n            else:\n                inputs[k] = v.to(device=device)\n\n    actions = model.generate_actions(\n        input_ids=inputs[\"input_ids\"],\n        image_input=inputs[\"image_input\"],\n        image_mask=inputs[\"image_mask\"],\n        proprio=proprio_t,\n        domain_id=domain_t,\n        steps=denoise_steps,\n    )  # [1, num_actions, dim_action]\n\n    return actions.squeeze(0).float().cpu().numpy()  # [num_actions, dim_action]\n\n\n# --- Wilson CI ---\n\ndef wilson_ci(successes, total, z=1.96):\n    if total == 0:\n        return 0.0, 0.0, 1.0\n    p = successes / total\n    denom = 1 + z ** 2 / total\n    center = (p + z ** 2 / (2 * total)) / denom\n    spread = z * np.sqrt((p * (1 - p) + z ** 2 / (4 * total)) / total) / denom\n    return center, max(0.0, center - spread), min(1.0, center + spread)\n\n\n# --- Main eval ---\n\ndef run_evaluation(args):\n    device = torch.device(f\"cuda:{args.gpu}\" if torch.cuda.is_available() else \"cpu\")\n    max_steps = args.max_steps or SUITE_MAX_STEPS.get(args.suite, 400)\n    output_path = Path(args.output or\n                       f\"eval_results/xvla_prune_k{args.keep_ratio}_{args.suite}.json\")\n\n    log.info(\"Suite: %s | Device: %s | keep_ratio=%.2f | Episodes/task: %d | Max steps: %d\",\n             args.suite, device, args.keep_ratio, args.num_episodes, max_steps)\n\n    np.random.seed(args.seed)\n    torch.manual_seed(args.seed)\n\n    # Load model\n    t0 = time_mod.time()\n    model, processor = load_model(args.model_path, device)\n    log.info(\"Model loaded in %.1fs\", time_mod.time() - t0)\n\n    # Detect vision token count before pruning\n    dummy_img = torch.randn(1, 3, 224, 224, device=device)\n    with torch.no_grad():\n        dummy_feats = model.vlm._encode_image(dummy_img)\n    n_vis_tokens = dummy_feats.shape[1]\n    k_tokens = max(1, int(args.keep_ratio * n_vis_tokens))\n    log.info(\"Vision tokens: %d total, keeping %d (%.0f%%)\",\n             n_vis_tokens, k_tokens, args.keep_ratio * 100)\n\n    # Install pruning\n    install_vision_pruning(model, args.keep_ratio)\n\n    # LIBERO setup\n    from libero.libero import benchmark\n    from lerobot.envs.libero import get_task_init_states, LiberoEnv\n\n    task_suite = benchmark.get_benchmark(args.suite)()\n    n_tasks = task_suite.n_tasks\n\n    if args.task_ids:\n        task_id_list = [int(x) for x in args.task_ids.split(\",\")]\n    else:\n        task_id_list = list(range(n_tasks))\n\n    eval_start = time_mod.time()\n    all_task_results = {}\n    total_successes = 0\n    total_episodes = 0\n\n    for task_id in task_id_list:\n        task = task_suite.get_task(task_id)\n        task_desc = task.language\n        log.info(\"--- Task %d: %s ---\", task_id, task_desc)\n\n        init_states = get_task_init_states(task_suite, task_id)\n\n        env = LiberoEnv(\n            task_suite=task_suite,\n            task_id=task_id,\n            task_suite_name=args.suite,\n            camera_name=\"agentview_image,robot0_eye_in_hand_image\",\n            obs_type=\"pixels_agent_pos\",\n            observation_width=256,\n            observation_height=256,\n            init_states=True,\n            n_envs=args.num_episodes,\n            episode_index=0,\n            control_mode=\"relative\",\n        )\n\n        task_successes = []\n        task_lengths = []\n\n        for ep in range(args.num_episodes):\n            obs, info = env.reset(seed=args.seed + task_id * 1000 + ep)\n            action_chunk = None\n            action_idx = 0\n            success = False\n\n            for step in range(max_steps):\n                if action_chunk is None or action_idx >= args.action_horizon:\n                    images = get_images_from_obs(obs)\n                    if not images:\n                        raise ValueError(f\"No images in obs keys: {list(obs.keys())}\")\n                    proprio = extract_proprio(obs)\n                    action_chunk = predict_actions(\n                        model, processor, images, task_desc, proprio,\n                        args.domain_id, device, args.denoise_steps,\n                    )\n                    action_idx = 0\n\n                action = action_chunk[action_idx]\n                action = np.clip(action, -1.0, 1.0).astype(np.float32)\n                action_idx += 1\n\n                obs, reward, terminated, truncated, info = env.step(action)\n\n                if info.get(\"is_success\", False):\n                    success = True\n                    break\n                if terminated or truncated:\n                    break\n\n            ep_steps = step + 1\n            task_successes.append(success)\n            task_lengths.append(ep_steps)\n            log.info(\"  Ep %d/%d | %s | steps=%d\",\n                     ep + 1, args.num_episodes,\n                     \"SUCCESS\" if success else \"FAIL\", ep_steps)\n\n        env.close()\n\n        n_succ = sum(task_successes)\n        n_total = len(task_successes)\n        sr = n_succ / n_total if n_total > 0 else 0.0\n\n        all_task_results[task_id] = {\n            \"task_id\": task_id,\n            \"task_description\": task_desc,\n            \"success_count\": n_succ,\n            \"total_episodes\": n_total,\n            \"success_rate\": round(sr, 4),\n            \"mean_steps\": round(float(np.mean(task_lengths)), 1),\n        }\n        total_successes += n_succ\n        total_episodes += n_total\n        log.info(\"  Task %d SR: %d/%d (%.1f%%)\", task_id, n_succ, n_total, sr * 100)\n\n    elapsed = time_mod.time() - eval_start\n    overall_sr = total_successes / total_episodes if total_episodes > 0 else 0.0\n    _, ci_lo, ci_hi = wilson_ci(total_successes, total_episodes)\n\n    log.info(\"=\" * 60)\n    log.info(\"SUITE: %s | MODEL: xvla | keep_ratio: %.2f\", args.suite, args.keep_ratio)\n    log.info(\"Overall SR: %d/%d (%.1f%%)\", total_successes, total_episodes, overall_sr * 100)\n    log.info(\"95%% Wilson CI: [%.1f%%, %.1f%%]\", ci_lo * 100, ci_hi * 100)\n    for tid, tr in sorted(all_task_results.items()):\n        log.info(\"  Task %d: %.0f%% (%d/%d) -- %s\",\n                 tid, tr[\"success_rate\"] * 100, tr[\"success_count\"],\n                 tr[\"total_episodes\"], tr[\"task_description\"])\n    log.info(\"Elapsed: %.1f min\", elapsed / 60)\n    log.info(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    summary = {\n        \"model\": \"xvla\",\n        \"model_path\": args.model_path,\n        \"mode\": \"l2_norm_prune\",\n        \"suite\": args.suite,\n        \"keep_ratio\": args.keep_ratio,\n        \"n_vision_tokens_original\": n_vis_tokens,\n        \"n_vision_tokens_kept\": k_tokens,\n        \"total_episodes\": total_episodes,\n        \"total_successes\": total_successes,\n        \"overall_success_rate\": round(overall_sr, 4),\n        \"wilson_ci_95\": [round(ci_lo, 4), round(ci_hi, 4)],\n        \"num_episodes_per_task\": args.num_episodes,\n        \"max_steps\": max_steps,\n        \"action_horizon\": args.action_horizon,\n        \"domain_id\": args.domain_id,\n        \"denoise_steps\": args.denoise_steps,\n        \"seed\": args.seed,\n        \"elapsed_seconds\": round(elapsed, 1),\n        \"per_task\": all_task_results,\n    }\n\n    output_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n    with open(output_path, \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(summary, f, indent=2)\n    log.info(\"Results saved to %s\", output_path)\n\n    return summary\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    args = parse_args()\n    run_evaluation(args)\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2521,"cache_read_input_tokens":107858,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":2521},"output_tokens":8,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"0efaa246-af0e-4c67-93a7-768cecc289df","uuid":"517d1fb2-5cb9-4e03-be14-c8108b58f5ca"}

benchmark.get_benchmark(args.suite)()\n    n_tasks = task_suite.n_tasks\n\n    if args.task_ids:\n        task_id_list = [int(x) for x in args.task_ids.split(\",\")]\n    else:\n        task_id_list = list(range(n_tasks))\n\n    eval_start = time_mod.time()\n    all_task_results = {}\n    total_successes = 0\n    total_episodes = 0\n\n    for task_id in task_id_list:\n        task = task_suite.get_task(task_id)\n        task_desc = task.language\n        log.info(\"--- Task %d: %s ---\", task_id, task_desc)\n\n        init_states = get_task_init_states(task_suite, task_id)\n\n        env = LiberoEnv(\n            task_suite=task_suite,\n            task_id=task_id,\n            task_suite_name=args.suite,\n            camera_name=\"agentview_image,robot0_eye_in_hand_image\",\n            obs_type=\"pixels_agent_pos\",\n            observation_width=256,\n            observation_height=256,\n            init_states=True,\n            n_envs=args.num_episodes,\n            episode_index=0,\n            control_mode=\"relative\",\n        )\n\n        task_successes = []\n        task_lengths = []\n\n        for ep in range(args.num_episodes):\n            obs, info = env.reset(seed=args.seed + task_id * 1000 + ep)\n            action_chunk = None\n            action_idx = 0\n            success = False\n\n            for step in range(max_steps):\n                if action_chunk is None or action_idx >= args.action_horizon:\n                    images = get_images_from_obs(obs)\n                    if not images:\n                        raise ValueError(f\"No images in obs keys: {list(obs.keys())}\")\n                    proprio = extract_proprio(obs)\n                    action_chunk = predict_actions(\n                        model, processor, images, task_desc, proprio,\n                        args.domain_id, device, args.denoise_steps,\n                    )\n                    action_idx = 0\n\n                action = action_chunk[action_idx]\n                action = np.clip(action, -1.0, 1.0).astype(np.float32)\n                action_idx += 1\n\n                obs, reward, terminated, truncated, info = env.step(action)\n\n                if info.get(\"is_success\", False):\n                    success = True\n                    break\n                if terminated or truncated:\n                    break\n\n            ep_steps = step + 1\n            task_successes.append(success)\n            task_lengths.append(ep_steps)\n            log.info(\"  Ep %d/%d | %s | steps=%d\",\n                     ep + 1, args.num_episodes,\n                     \"SUCCESS\" if success else \"FAIL\", ep_steps)\n\n        env.close()\n\n        n_succ = sum(task_successes)\n        n_total = len(task_successes)\n        sr = n_succ / n_total if n_total > 0 else 0.0\n\n        all_task_results[task_id] = {\n            \"task_id\": task_id,\n            \"task_description\": task_desc,\n            \"success_count\": n_succ,\n            \"total_episodes\": n_total,\n            \"success_rate\": round(sr, 4),\n            \"mean_steps\": round(float(np.mean(task_lengths)), 1),\n        }\n        total_successes += n_succ\n        total_episodes += n_total\n        log.info(\"  Task %d SR: %d/%d (%.1f%%)\", task_id, n_succ, n_total, sr * 100)\n\n    elapsed = time_mod.time() - eval_start\n    overall_sr = total_successes / total_episodes if total_episodes > 0 else 0.0\n    _, ci_lo, ci_hi = wilson_ci(total_successes, total_episodes)\n\n    log.info(\"=\" * 60)\n    log.info(\"SUITE: %s | MODEL: xvla | keep_ratio: %.2f\", args.suite, args.keep_ratio)\n    log.info(\"Overall SR: %d/%d (%.1f%%)\", total_successes, total_episodes, overall_sr * 100)\n    log.info(\"95%% Wilson CI: [%.1f%%, %.1f%%]\", ci_lo * 100, ci_hi * 100)\n    for tid, tr in sorted(all_task_results.items()):\n        log.info(\"  Task %d: %.0f%% (%d/%d) -- %s\",\n                 tid, tr[\"success_rate\"] * 100, tr[\"success_count\"],\n                 tr[\"total_episodes\"], tr[\"task_description\"])\n    log.info(\"Elapsed: %.1f min\", elapsed / 60)\n    log.info(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    summary = {\n        \"model\": \"xvla\",\n        \"model_path\": args.model_path,\n        \"mode\": \"l2_norm_prune\",\n        \"suite\": args.suite,\n        \"keep_ratio\": args.keep_ratio,\n        \"n_vision_tokens_original\": n_vis_tokens,\n        \"n_vision_tokens_kept\": k_tokens,\n        \"total_episodes\": total_episodes,\n        \"total_successes\": total_successes,\n        \"overall_success_rate\": round(overall_sr, 4),\n        \"wilson_ci_95\": [round(ci_lo, 4), round(ci_hi, 4)],\n        \"num_episodes_per_task\": args.num_episodes,\n        \"max_steps\": max_steps,\n        \"action_horizon\": args.action_horizon,\n        \"domain_id\": args.domain_id,\n        \"denoise_steps\": args.denoise_steps,\n        \"seed\": args.seed,\n        \"elapsed_seconds\": round(elapsed, 1),\n        \"per_task\": all_task_results,\n    }\n\n    output_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n    with open(output_path, \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(summary, f, indent=2)\n    log.info(\"Results saved to %s\", output_path)\n\n    return summary\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    args = parse_args()\n    run_evaluation(args)\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null,"userModified":false}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_0119KJBnFqH1XNzR9qg6ULg3","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01TdHQRdhsgmM15GbnosqfWz","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-be-idea-gen-0515-2/projects/importance_margin_vla_compress/artifacts/run_xvla_prune_sweep.sh","content":"#!/bin/bash\n# X-VLA Pruning Sweep on LIBERO-Object\n# Runs keep_ratio values sequentially on GPU 0\n\nset -e\n\nexport MUJOCO_GL=egl\nexport CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=0\nexport TRANSFORMERS_OFFLINE=1\nexport HF_HUB_OFFLINE=1\nexport HF_HOME=/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache\n\nsource /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh\nconda activate base\n\ncd /root/autodl-tmp/importance_margin_vla_compress\n\nMODEL_PATH=\"/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache/models--2toINF--X-VLA-Libero/snapshots/129e71460678b7236cee6fc9707f09d9fa0c3590\"\nSUITE=\"libero_object\"\nEPISODES=50\nSEED=42\nDOMAIN_ID=0\n\nK_VALUES=\"1.0 0.95 0.9 0.7 0.5 0.3 0.2 0.1\"\n\necho \"===== X-VLA Pruning Sweep =====\"\necho \"Suite: $SUITE | Episodes/task: $EPISODES | Seed: $SEED\"\necho \"K values: $K_VALUES\"\necho \"\"\n\nfor K in $K_VALUES; {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_01GyGZRXGaKJadoyZFCwPcYS","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01RCvfM5gCSGxhnX7NEaFpxz","name":"mcp__agent-ml-research__ssh_execute","input":{"server":"westd-38929","command":"cat > /root/autodl-tmp/importance_margin_vla_compress/eval_xvla_pruning_libero.py << 'SCRIPT_EOF'\n\"\"\"X-VLA Vision Token Pruning Evaluation on LIBERO.\n\nPrunes vision tokens by L2 norm after Florence2's image encoder + linear projection,\nbefore they enter the Florence2 language encoder. Evaluates on LIBERO-Object.\n\nUsage:\n    CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=0 MUJOCO_GL=egl python eval_xvla_pruning_libero.py \\\n        --model_path 2toINF/X-VLA-Libero \\\n        --suite libero_object \\\n        --keep_ratio 0.5 \\\n        --num_episodes 50 \\\n        --gpu 0\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport argparse\nimport json\nimport logging\nimport os\nimport sys\nimport time as time_mod\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom functools import wraps\n\nos.environ.setdefault(\"MUJOCO_GL\", \"egl\")\n\nimport numpy as np\nimport torch\nfrom PIL import Image\n\nsys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent))\n\nlogging.basicConfig(\n    level=logging.INFO,\n    format=\"%(asctime)s [%(levelname)s] %(message)s\",\n    datefmt=\"%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S\",\n)\nlog = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n\nSUITE_MAX_STEPS = {\n    \"libero_spatial\": 280,\n    \"libero_object\": 280,\n    \"libero_goal\": 300,\n    \"libero_10\": 520,\n    \"libero_90\": 400,\n}\n\n\ndef parse_args():\n    p = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=\"X-VLA pruning eval on LIBERO\")\n    p.add_argument(\"--model_path\", type=str, default=\"2toINF/X-VLA-Libero\",\n                   help=\"HuggingFace model path or local dir\")\n    p.add_argument(\"--suite\", type=str, default=\"libero_object\",\n                   choices=list(SUITE_MAX_STEPS.keys()))\n    p.add_argument(\"--keep_ratio\", type=float, default=1.0,\n                   help=\"Fraction of vision tokens to keep (1.0 = no pruning)\")\n    p.add_argument(\"--num_episodes\", type=int, default=50)\n    p.add_argument(\"--gpu\", type=int, default=0)\n    p.add_argument(\"--seed\", type=int, default=42)\n    p.add_argument(\"--action_horizon\", type=int, default=10,\n                   help=\"How many actions from each chunk to execute before re-planning\")\n    p.add_argument(\"--max_steps\", type=int, default=None)\n    p.add_argument(\"--output\", type=str, default=None)\n    p.add_argument(\"--task_ids\", type=str, default=None)\n    p.add_argument(\"--domain_id\", type=int, default=0)\n    p.add_argument(\"--denoise_steps\", type=int, default=10,\n                   help=\"Flow matching denoising steps\")\n    return p.parse_args()\n\n\n# --- Pruning hook ---\n\ndef install_vision_pruning(model, keep_ratio: float):\n    \"\"\"Monkey-patch _encode_image to prune vision tokens by L2 norm.\"\"\"\n    if keep_ratio >= 1.0:\n        log.info(\"keep_ratio=%.2f, no pruning applied\", keep_ratio)\n        return\n\n    original_encode = model.vlm._encode_image\n\n    @wraps(original_encode)\n    def pruned_encode(pixel_values):\n        features = original_encode(pixel_values)  # [B, N, D]\n        N = features.shape[1]\n        k = max(1, int(keep_ratio * N))\n        if k >= N:\n            return features\n        norms = features.norm(dim=-1)  # [B, N]\n        _, indices = norms.topk(k, dim=-1)\n        indices_sorted = indices.sort(dim=-1).values\n        pruned = features.gather(\n            1, indices_sorted.unsqueeze(-1).expand(-1, -1, features.shape[-1])\n        )\n        return pruned\n\n    model.vlm._encode_image = pruned_encode\n    log.info(\"Vision pruning installed: keep_ratio=%.2f\", keep_ratio)\n\n\n# --- Model loading ---\n\ndef load_model(model_path: str, device: torch.device):\n    from transformers import AutoModel, AutoProcessor\n\n    log.info(\"Loading X-VLA from %s\", model_path)\n    model = AutoModel.from_pretrained(\n        model_path,\n        torch_dtype=torch.float32,\n        trust_remote_code=True,\n        low_cpu_mem_usage=True,\n    )\n    model = model.to(device).eval()\n    processor = AutoProcessor.from_pretrained(model_path, trust_remote_code=True)\n\n    n_params = sum(p.numel() for p in model.parameters())\n    log.info(\"Model loaded: %.1fM params, dtype=%s\", n_params / 1e6, model.dtype)\n    return model, processor\n\n\n# --- Observation processing ---\n\ndef quat_to_axis_angle(quat):\n    q = quat.astype(np.float64)\n    w = np.clip(q[3], -1.0, 1.0)\n    den = np.sqrt(max(1.0 - w * w, 0.0))\n    if den > 1e-10:\n        angle = 2.0 * np.arccos(w)\n        axis = q[:3] / den\n        return (axis * angle).astype(np.float32)\n    return np.zeros(3, dtype=np.float32)\n\n\ndef extract_proprio(obs):\n    \"\"\"Extract 7D proprio state: [pos(3), axis_angle(3), gripper(1)].\"\"\"\n    rs = obs[\"robot_state\"]\n    eef_pos = np.asarray(rs[\"eef\"][\"pos\"], dtype=np.float32)\n    eef_quat = np.asarray(rs[\"eef\"][\"quat\"], dtype=np.float64)\n    gripper_qpos = np.asarray(rs[\"gripper\"][\"qpos\"], dtype=np.float32)\n    eef_aa = quat_to_axis_angle(eef_quat)\n    return np.concatenate([eef_pos, eef_aa, gripper_qpos[:1]])\n\n\ndef get_images_from_obs(obs):\n    \"\"\"Extract camera images from LIBERO observation.\"\"\"\n    images = []\n    for cam_name in [\"agentview_image\", \"robot0_eye_in_hand_image\"]:\n        img = obs.get(\"pixels\", {}).get(cam_name, None)\n        if img is None:\n            for k, v in obs.items():\n                if cam_name in k and isinstance(v, np.ndarray):\n                    img = v\n                    break\n        if img is not None:\n            if img.dtype in (np.float32, np.float64):\n                img = (np.clip(img, 0, 1) * 255).astype(np.uint8)\n            images.append(Image.fromarray(img))\n    return images\n\n\n# --- Action prediction ---\n\n@torch.no_grad()\ndef predict_actions(model, processor, images, task_desc, proprio, domain_id, device,\n                    denoise_steps=10):\n    \"\"\"Get action chunk from X-VLA model.\"\"\"\n    inputs = processor(images=images, language_instruction=task_desc)\n\n    proprio_t = torch.tensor(proprio, dtype=torch.float32).unsqueeze(0).to(device)\n    domain_t = torch.tensor([domain_id], dtype=torch.long).to(device)\n\n    for k, v in inputs.items():\n        if isinstance(v, torch.Tensor):\n            if v.is_floating_point():\n                inputs[k] = v.to(device=device, dtype=torch.float32)\n            else:\n                inputs[k] = v.to(device=device)\n\n    actions = model.generate_actions(\n        input_ids=inputs[\"input_ids\"],\n        image_input=inputs[\"image_input\"],\n        image_mask=inputs[\"image_mask\"],\n        proprio=proprio_t,\n        domain_id=domain_t,\n        steps=denoise_steps,\n    )  # [1, num_actions, dim_action]\n\n    return actions.squeeze(0).float().cpu().numpy()  # [num_actions, dim_action]\n\n\n# --- Wilson CI ---\n\ndef wilson_ci(successes, total, z=1.96):\n    if total == 0:\n        return 0.0, 0.0, 1.0\n    p = successes / total\n    denom = 1 + z ** 2 / total\n    center = (p + z ** 2 / (2 * total)) / denom\n    spread = z * np.sqrt((p * (1 - p) + z ** 2 / (4 * total)) / total) / denom\n    return center, max(0.0, center - spread), min(1.0, center + spread)\n\n\n# --- Main eval ---\n\ndef run_evaluation(args):\n    device = torch.device(f\"cuda:{args.gpu}\" if torch.cuda.is_available() else \"cpu\")\n    max_steps = args.max_steps or SUITE_MAX_STEPS.get(args.suite, 400)\n    output_path = Path(args.output or\n                       f\"eval_results/xvla_prune_k{args.keep_ratio}_{args.suite}.json\")\n\n    log.info(\"Suite: %s | Device: %s | keep_ratio=%.2f | Episodes/task: %d | Max steps: %d\",\n             args.suite, device, args.keep_ratio, args.num_episodes, max_steps)\n\n    np.random.seed(args.seed)\n    torch.manual_seed(args.seed)\n\n    # Load model\n    t0 = time_mod.time()\n    model, processor = load_model(args.model_path, device)\n    log.info(\"Model loaded in %.1fs\", time_mod.time() - t0)\n\n    # Detect vision token count before pruning\n    dummy_img = torch.randn(1, 3, 224, 224, device=device)\n    with torch.no_grad():\n        dummy_feats = model.vlm._encode_image(dummy_img)\n    n_vis_tokens = dummy_feats.shape[1]\n    k_tokens = max(1, int(args.keep_ratio * n_vis_tokens))\n    log.info(\"Vision tokens: %d total, keeping %d (%.0f%%)\",\n             n_vis_tokens, k_tokens, args.keep_ratio * 100)\n\n    # Install pruning\n    install_vision_pruning(model, args.keep_ratio)\n\n    # LIBERO setup\n    from libero.libero import benchmark\n    from lerobot.envs.libero import get_task_init_states, LiberoEnv\n\n    task_suite = benchmark.get_benchmark(args.suite)()\n    n_tasks = task_suite.n_tasks\n\n    if args.task_ids:\n        task_id_list = [int(x) for x in args.task_ids.split(\",\")]\n    else:\n        task_id_list = list(range(n_tasks))\n\n    eval_start = time_mod.time()\n    all_task_results = {}\n    total_successes = 0\n    total_episodes = 0\n\n    for task_id in task_id_list:\n        task = task_suite.get_task(task_id)\n        task_desc = task.language\n        log.info(\"--- Task %d: %s ---\", task_id, task_desc)\n\n        init_states = get_task_init_states(task_suite, task_id)\n\n        env = LiberoEnv(\n            task_suite=task_suite,\n            task_id=task_id,\n            task_suite_name=args.suite,\n            camera_name=\"agentview_image,robot0_eye_in_hand_image\",\n            obs_type=\"pixels_agent_pos\",\n            observation_width=256,\n            observation_height=256,\n            init_states=True,\n            n_envs=args.num_episodes,\n            episode_index=0,\n            control_mode=\"relative\",\n        )\n\n        task_successes = []\n        task_lengths = []\n\n        for ep in range(args.num_episodes):\n            obs, info = env.reset(seed=args.seed + task_id * 1000 + ep)\n            action_chunk = None\n            action_idx = 0\n            success = False\n\n            for step in range(max_steps):\n                if action_chunk is None or action_idx >= args.action_horizon:\n                    images = get_images_from_obs(obs)\n                    if not images:\n                        raise ValueError(f\"No images in obs keys: {list(obs.keys())}\")\n                    proprio = extract_proprio(obs)\n                    action_chunk = predict_actions(\n                        model, processor, images, task_desc, proprio,\n                        args.domain_id, device, args.denoise_steps,\n                    )\n                    action_idx = 0\n\n                action = action_chunk[action_idx]\n                action = np.clip(action, -1.0, 1.0).astype(np.float32)\n                action_idx += 1\n\n                obs, reward, terminated, truncated, info = env.step(action)\n\n                if info.get(\"is_success\", False):\n                    success = True\n                    break\n                if terminated or truncated:\n                    break\n\n            ep_steps = step + 1\n            task_successes.append(success)\n            task_lengths.append(ep_steps)\n            log.info(\"  Ep %d/%d | %s | steps=%d\",\n                     ep + 1, args.num_episodes,\n                     \"SUCCESS\" if success else \"FAIL\", ep_steps)\n\n        env.close()\n\n        n_succ = sum(task_successes)\n        n_total = len(task_successes)\n        sr = n_succ / n_total if n_total > 0 else 0.0\n\n        all_task_results[task_id] = {\n            \"task_id\": task_id,\n            \"task_description\": task_desc,\n            \"success_count\": n_succ,\n            \"total_episodes\": n_total,\n            \"success_rate\": round(sr, 4),\n            \"mean_steps\": round(float(np.mean(task_lengths)), 1),\n        }\n        total_successes += n_succ\n        total_episodes += n_total\n        log.info(\"  Task %d SR: %d/%d (%.1f%%)\", task_id, n_succ, n_total, sr * 100)\n\n    elapsed = time_mod.time() - eval_start\n    overall_sr = total_successes / total_episodes if total_episodes > 0 else 0.0\n    _, ci_lo, ci_hi = wilson_ci(total_successes, total_episodes)\n\n    log.info(\"=\" * 60)\n AutoModel, AutoProcessor\n\nmodel_path = '/root/autodl-tmp/X-VLA-Libero'\nprint('Loading model...')\nmodel = AutoModel.from_pretrained(model_path, torch_dtype=torch.float32, trust_remote_code=True, {feats.shape}')\n\n# Load processor\nprocessor = AutoProcessor.from_pretrained(model_path, trust_remote_code=True)\nprint(f'Processor loaded: {type(processor).__name__}')\n\n# Quick inference language_instruction='pick up the red object')\nprint(f'Processor output keys: {list(inputs.keys())}')\nfor k, v in inputs.items():\n    if isinstance(v, torch.Tensor):\n        print(f'  {k}: {v.shape} {v.dtype}')\n\n# Test generate_actions\nfor k, v in inputs.items():\n    if isinstance(v, torch.Tensor):\n        if v.is_floating_point():\n            inputs[k] = v.to(device=device, dtype=torch.float32)\n        else:\n            inputs[k] = v.to(device=device)\n\nproprio = torch.zeros(1, 7, device=device, dtype=torch.float32)\ndomain_id = torch.tensor([0], dtype=torch.long, device=device)\n\nwith torch.no_grad():\n    actions = model.generate_actions(\n        input_ids=inputs['input_ids'],\n        image_input=inputs['image_input'],\n        image_mask=inputs['image_mask'],\n        proprio=proprio,\n        domain_id=domain_id,\n        steps=10,\n    )\nprint(f'Actions shape: {actions.shape}')\nprint(f'Action range: {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_011yKuevT7Ai6iWnfNPg4GTj","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"thinking","thinking":"The issue is that `_supports_sdpa` is a property that accesses `self.language_model._supports_sdpa`. But during `__init__`, `self.language_model` hasn't been set yet when `super().__init__(config)` is called.\n\nIn transformers 5.x, `PreTrainedModel.__init__` calls `_check_and_adjust_attn_implementation` which tries to access `_supports_sdpa` before the subclass has finished setting up its attributes.\n\nThe fix: make `_supports_sdpa` and `_supports_flash_attn_2` return a default value when `language_model` isn't available yet.\n\n\nNow I'm modifying these properties to gracefully handle the case where the language model hasn't been initialized yet, returning sensible defaults during the early initialization {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"type":"text","text":"This session is being continued from a previous conversation that ran out of context. The summary below covers the earlier portion of the conversation.\n\nSummary:\n1. Primary Request and Intent:\n   The user requested running X-VLA LIBERO Pruning Eval on server westd-38929:0. Specific steps:\n   - Environment check on westd-38929\n   - Download X-VLA-Libero weights (from HuggingFace 2toINF/X-VLA-Libero)\n   - Reference existing eval scripts in the project\n   - Write `eval_xvla_pruning_libero.py` implementing vision token pruning by L2 norm\n   - Run eval sweep with keep_ratio k values: 0.1, 0.2, 0.3, 0.5, 0.7, 0.9, 0.95 (50 eps/task × 10 tasks each)\n   - Optionally run entropy ratio analysis\n   - Constraints: only cuda:0, conda base env activation\n   - Context: X-VLA offline analysis already completed showing 64 vision tokens (actually 50), Linear projection connector, k=0.3 only +0.14% loss. This eval confirms pruning impact on actual success rate.\n\n2. Key Technical Concepts:\n   - **X-VLA Architecture**: Florence2 encoder-only backbone (DaViT vision + BART text encoder) + SoftPromptedTransformer (flow-matching action head, 24 layers, hidden_size=1024)\n   - **Vision Token Pipeline**: DaViT(224×224 input → 7×7=49 spatial tokens) → 2D pos embed → spatial_avg_pool(1 token) + temporal_avg_pool(49 tokens) → cat → linear projection (2048→1024) → LayerNorm = **50 vision tokens total**\n   - **forward_vlm flow**: Flatten multi-view images → _encode_image per valid view → first view merged with text → Florence2 encoder → vlm_features [B, T_enc, D] + aux_visual_inputs [B, (V-1)*N, D]\n   - **generate_actions flow**: forward_vlm → iterative flow-matching denoising (10 steps) through SoftPromptedTransformer → postprocess (sigmoid on gripper)\n   - **Pruning strategy**: Monkey-patch `model.vlm._encode_image` to prune vision tokens by L2 norm after linear projection + LayerNorm, before they enter Florence2 encoder\n   - **Action space**: Config says `action_mode: \"ee6d\"` but actual dim_action=20 (not 7) — needs investigation\n   - **XVLAProcessor**: CLIPImageProcessor (224×224, ImageNet normalization) + BartTokenizer, pads to 3 views, outputs input_ids [B,L], image_input [B,3,C,H,W], image_mask [B,3]\n\n3. Files and Code Sections:\n\n   - **`/root/autodl-tmp/X-VLA-Libero/`** (model directory on server)\n     - Contains all model files: config.json, modeling_xvla.py, modeling_florence2.py, configuration_xvla.py, configuration_florence2.py, processing_xvla.py, action_hub.py, transformer.py, tokenizer.json, vocab.json, preprocessor_config.json, model.safetensors (3.3GB)\n     - Patched configuration_florence2.py: `getattr(self, \"forced_bos_token_id\", None)` fix\n     - Patched modeling_florence2.py: Added `hasattr(self, 'language_model')` guards on `_supports_sdpa` and `_supports_flash_attn_2` properties\n\n   - **`/root/autodl-tmp/importance_margin_vla_compress/eval_xvla_pruning_libero.py`** (eval script on server)\n     - Core pruning implementation via monkey-patching:\n     ```python\n     def install_vision_pruning(model, keep_ratio: float):\n         if keep_ratio >= 1.0:\n             return\n         original_encode = model.vlm._encode_image\n         @wraps(original_encode)\n         def pruned_encode(pixel_values):\n             features = original_encode(pixel_values)  # [B, N, D]\n             N = features.shape[1]\n             k = max(1, int(keep_ratio * N))\n             if k >= N:\n                 return features\n             norms = features.norm(dim=-1)  # [B, N]\n             _, indices = norms.topk(k, dim=-1)\n             indices_sorted = indices.sort(dim=-1).values\n             pruned = features.gather(\n                 1, indices_sorted.unsqueeze(-1).expand(-1, -1, features.shape[-1])\n             )\n             return pruned\n         model.vlm._encode_image = pruned_encode\n     ```\n     - **CRITICAL BUG**: `extract_proprio` returns 7D `[pos(3), axis_angle(3), gripper(1)]` but model expects 20D (dim_action=20)\n     - Action prediction uses `model.generate_actions()` which returns [1, 30, 20] not [1, 30, 7]\n     - LIBERO env outputs 7D actions, so action mapping from 20D X-VLA output to 7D LIBERO is needed\n\n   - **`/root/autodl-tmp/importance_margin_vla_compress/run_xvla_prune_sweep.sh`** (sweep script on server)\n     - Runs k values: 1.0, 0.95, 0.9, 0.7, 0.5, 0.3, 0.2, 0.1 sequentially\n     - MODEL_PATH=\"/root/autodl-tmp/X-VLA-Libero\"\n     - Uses MUJOCO_GL=egl, CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=0, conda base\n\n   - **Existing reference scripts on server**:\n     - `eval_smart_compress_libero.py`: SmolVLA eval pattern using LiberoEnv from lerobot.envs.libero\n     - `eval_openvla_acis_prune.py`: OpenVLA attention-based pruning eval on LIBERO\n     - Both use `from libero.libero import benchmark` and `from lerobot.envs.libero import get_task_init_states, LiberoEnv`\n\n   - **Key X-VLA model code** (modeling_xvla.py forward_vlm):\n     ```python\n     def forward_vlm(self, input_ids, pixel_values, image_mask):\n         B, V = pixel_values.shape[:2]\n         flat_mask = image_mask.view(-1).to(torch.bool)\n         flat_images = pixel_values.flatten(0, 1)\n         valid_images = flat_images[flat_mask]\n         valid_feats = self.vlm._encode_image(valid_images)  # [#valid, N, D]\n         N, D = valid_feats.shape[1:]\n         image_features = valid_feats.new_zeros((B * V, N, D))\n         image_features[flat_mask] = valid_feats\n         image_features = image_features.view(B, V, N, D)\n         inputs_embeds = self.vlm.get_input_embeddings()(input_ids)\n         merged_embeds, attention_mask = self.vlm._merge_input_ids_with_image_features(\n             image_features[:, 0], inputs_embeds)\n         enc_out = self.vlm.language_model.model.encoder(\n             attention_mask=attention_mask, inputs_embeds=merged_embeds)[0]\n         aux_visual_inputs = image_features[:, 1:].reshape(B, -1, D)\n         return {\"vlm_features\": enc_out, \"aux_visual_inputs\": aux_visual_inputs}\n     ```\n\n   - **action_hub.py EE6DActionSpace**:\n     ```python\n     @register_action(\"ee6d\")\n     class EE6DActionSpace(BaseActionSpace):\n         dim_action = 7\n         gripper_idx = (6,)\n     ```\n     But AGIBOTEE6DActionSpace has dim_action=20, gripper_idx=(9,19).\n\n4.
